variables
{
	global:
		0: _extendedGlobalCollection
		1: pfI1
		2: pfI2
		3: _arrayConstructor

	player:
		0: _extendedPlayerCollection
		1: pfChaseTarget
		2: pfTestI
}

subroutines
{
	0: pfUpdateNodeMeta
	1: pfWalkToDestination
	2: pfProgressEventPlayer
	3: pfLookupNextHop
	4: pfReadNextHopChar
	5: pfRecalibrate
	6: pfLookAtDestination
}

rule("【PF Decompress】Decompress map")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Global;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3] == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Lock the pathfinder until the map is ready. pfIterationsThisTick = -99 is kept for older code that checks it."
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[10] = False;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[2] = -99;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4] = Custom String("{0}{1}", Custom String("{0}{1}{2}", Custom String("{0}{1}{2}", Custom String(
			"{0}{1}{2}", Custom String(
			" !#$%&'()*+,-./:;<=>?@[]^_`|~ ¡¢£¤¥¦§¨©«¬­®¯°±´¶·¸»¿×÷˂˃˄˅˒˓˔˕˖˗˘˙˚˛˜˝˞˟˥˦˧˨˩˪˫˭˯˰˱˲˳˴˵˶˷˸˹˺˻˼˽"),
			Custom String(
			"˾˿̴̵̶̷̸̡̢̧̨̛̖̗̘̙̜̝̞̟̠̣̤̥̦̩̪̫̬̭̮̯̰̱̲̳̹̺̻̼͇͈͉͍͎̀́̂̃̄̅̆̇̈̉̊̋̌̍̎̏̐̑̒̓̔̽̾̿̀́͂̓̈́͆͊͋͌̕̚ͅ͏͓͔͕͖͙͚͐͑͒͗͛ͣͤͥͦͧͨͩͪͫͬͭͮͯ͘͜͟͢͝͞͠͡͵͸͹;΀΁΂΃΄΅·΋΍΢"),
			Custom String(
			"϶҂҃҄҅҆҇҈҉԰՗՘՚՛՜՝՞՟։֊֋֌֍֎֏֐ְֱֲֳִֵֶַָֹֺֻּֽ֑֖֛֢֣֤֥֦֧֪֚֭֮֒֓֔֕֗֘֙֜֝֞֟֠֡֨֩֫֬֯־ֿ׀ׁׂ׃ׅׄ׆ׇ׈׉׊׋׌׍׎׏׫׬׭׮׳״׵׶׷׸׹׺׻׼׽׾׿؀؁؂؃؄؅؆؇؈؉؊؋،؍؎؏ؐؑؒؓؔؕ")),
			Custom String(
			"ؘؙؚؖؗ؛؜؝؞؟ًٌٍَُِّْٕٖٜٟٓٔٗ٘ٙٚٛٝٞ٪٫٬٭ٰ۔ۖۗۘۙۚۛۜ۝۞ۣ۟۠ۡۢۤۧۨ۩۪ۭ۫۬۽۾܀܁܂܃܄܅܆܇܈܉܊܋܌܍܎܏ܑܱܴܷܸܹܻܼܾ݂݄݆݈ܰܲܳܵܶܺܽܿ݀݁݃݅݇݉݊݋݌ަާިީުޫެޭޮޯް޲޳޴޵޶޷޸޹޺޻"),
			Custom String(
			"޼޽޾޿߲߫߬߭߮߯߰߱߳߶߷߸߹߻߼߽߾߿ࠖࠗ࠘࠙ࠛࠜࠝࠞࠟࠠࠡࠢࠣࠥࠦࠧࠩࠪࠫࠬ࠭࠮࠯࠰࠱࠲࠳࠴࠵࠶࠷࠸࠹࠺࠻࠼࠽࠾࠿࡙࡚࡛࡜࡝࡞࡟࡫࡬࡭࡮࡯ࡰࡱࡲࡳࡴࡵࡶࡷࡸࡹࡺࡻࡼࡽࡾࡿࢀࢁࢂࢃࢄࢅࢆࢇ࢈ࢉࢊࢋࢌࢍࢎ࢏࢐࢑࢒࢓࢔࢕࢖࢙࢚࢛ࢗ࢘࢜࢝࢞࢟ࢵࣈࣉ࣊࣋࣌࣍")),
			Custom String(
			"࣏࣐࣑࣒࣓࣎ࣔࣕࣖࣗࣘࣙࣚࣛࣜࣝࣞࣟ࣠࣡࣢ࣰࣱࣲࣣࣦࣩ࣭࣮࣯ࣶࣹࣺࣤࣥࣧࣨ࣪࣫࣬ࣳࣴࣵࣷࣸࣻࣼࣽࣾࣿऀँंःऺऻ़ािीुूृॄॅॆेैॉॊोौ्ॎॏ॒॑॓॔ॕॖॗॢॣ।॥॰ঁংঃ঄঍঎঑঒঩঱঳঴঵঺঻়ািীুূৃৄ৅৆েৈ৉৊োৌ্৏৐৑৒৓৔৕৖ৗ"),
			Custom String(
			"৘৙৚৛৞ৢৣ৤৥৲৳৺৻৽৾৿਀ਁਂਃ਄਋਌਍਎਑਒਩਱਴਷਺਻਼਽ਾਿੀੁੂ੃੄੅੆ੇੈ੉੊ੋੌ੍੎੏੐ੑ੒੓੔੕੖੗੘੝੟੠੡੢੣੤੥ੰੱੵ੶੷੸੹੺੻੼੽੾੿઀ઁંઃ઄઎઒઩઱઴઺઻઼ાિીુૂૃૄૅ૆ેૈૉ૊ોૌ્૎૏૑૒૓૔૕૖૗૘૙૚૛૜૝૞")),
			Custom String(
			"૟ૢૣ૤૥૰૱૲૳૴૵૶૷૸ૺૻૼ૽૾૿଀ଁଂଃ଄଍଎଑଒଩଱଴଺଻଼ାିୀୁୂୃୄ୅୆େୈ୉୊ୋୌ୍୎୏୐୑୒୓୔୕ୖୗ୘୙୚୛୞ୢୣ୤୥୰୸୹୺୻୼୽୾୿஀஁ஂ஄஋஌஍஑஖஗஘஛஝஠஡஢஥஦஧஫஬஭஺஻஼஽ாிீுூ௃௄௅ெேை௉ொோௌ்௎௏௑௒௓௔௕"));
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[0] = Empty Array;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[1] = Empty Array;
		"Decompress nodes: 6 chars per node, 2 base 1024 chars per axis."
		For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])), 1);
			For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])[Global.pfI1]), 6);
				Global._extendedGlobalCollection[0] = Append To Array(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection), Vector((Index Of String Char(
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])[Global.pfI1], Global.pfI2))
					* 1024 + Index Of String Char(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])
					[Global.pfI1], Global.pfI2 + 1)) - 524288) / 100, (Index Of String Char(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(
					First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])[Global.pfI1], Global.pfI2 + 2)) * 1024 + Index Of String Char(
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])[Global.pfI1],
					Global.pfI2 + 3)) - 524288) / 100, (Index Of String Char(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(First Of(
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])[Global.pfI1], Global.pfI2 + 4)) * 1024 + Index Of String Char(
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3])[Global.pfI1],
					Global.pfI2 + 5)) - 524288) / 100));
				Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
			End;
		End;
		"Decompress neighbors"
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[5] = 0;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6] = Empty Array;
		For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3][1]), 1);
			For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3][1][Global.pfI1]), 1);
				Global._extendedGlobalCollection[7] = Index Of String Char(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3][1][Global.pfI1], Global.pfI2));
				"Next is the neighbor."
				If(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[5] == 0);
					"Current node is terminated."
					If(!Global._extendedGlobalCollection[7]);
						Global._extendedGlobalCollection[1] = Append To Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[1], Array(
							Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6]));
						Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6] = Empty Array;
					Else;
						Global._extendedGlobalCollection[5] = 1;
						Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6] = Append To Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6], Array(Array(
							Global._extendedGlobalCollection[7] - 1, Empty Array)));
					End;
				Else If(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[5] == 1);
					"Current neighbor is terminated."
					If(!Global._extendedGlobalCollection[7]);
						Global._extendedGlobalCollection[5] = 0;
					Else;
						Global._arrayConstructor = Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6];
						Global._arrayConstructor[Count Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6]) - 1] = Array(First Of(Last Of(
							Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6])), Append To Array(Last Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6])[1],
							Global._extendedGlobalCollection[7] - 1));
						Global._extendedGlobalCollection[6] = Global._arrayConstructor;
					End;
				End;
				If(!(Global.pfI2 % 20));
					Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
				End;
			End;
			Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
		End;
		"Connected component of every node (3rd char of each 3 char node meta entry, 42 entries per string)."
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8] = Empty Array;
		For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)), 1);
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8] = Append To Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8], Index Of String Char(
				Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3][3][Round To Integer(Global.pfI1 / 42,
				Down)], Global.pfI1 % 42 * 3 + 2)));
			If(!(Global.pfI1 % 20));
				Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
			End;
		End;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9] = Mapped Array(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection), Current Array Index);
		"Node list of every connected part, built once, so goal selection does not have to filter every node."
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[11] = Empty Array;
		For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Last Of(Sorted Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8], Current Array Element)) + 1, 1);
			Global._arrayConstructor = Global._extendedGlobalCollection[11];
			Global._arrayConstructor[Global.pfI1] = Filtered Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9],
				Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Current Array Element] == Global.pfI1);
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection[11] = Global._arrayConstructor;
			Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
		End;
		"The compression table stays loaded: Floyd-Warshall rows are read on demand while bots walk."
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[2] = 0;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[10] = True;
	}
}

rule("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Update node meta")
{
	event
	{
		Subroutine;
		pfUpdateNodeMeta;
	}

	actions
	{
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[7] = Total Time Elapsed;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[6] = Position Of(Event Player);
	}
}

rule("☆【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Walk to destination")
{
	event
	{
		Subroutine;
		pfWalkToDestination;
	}

	actions
	{
		Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Position Of(Event Player),
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] == -1 ? First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection) : First Of(
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]]), Distance Between(Position Of(Event Player),
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] == -1 ? First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection) : First Of(
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]]) > 0.400 && (
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] != -1 || !(!Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] || Distance Between(First Of(
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection), Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2]) > 5)), To World, Replace existing throttle,
			Direction and Magnitude);
	}
}

rule("【PF Core】Update & Execute")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection) == True;
		(!Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] || Distance Between(First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection),
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2]) > 5) == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Wait until a map has been loaded."
		If(!Global._extendedGlobalCollection[10]);
			Wait Until(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[10], 99999);
		End;
		"Random delay of up to 0.3 seconds, so bots chasing the same target do not all sort nodes on the same frame."
		Wait(Random Real(0, 0.300), Ignore Condition);
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] = First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection);
		"Start node: keep the node already being walked to, otherwise use the nearest visible node."
		If(!Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3] || Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] == -1);
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] = First Of(Sorted Array(Array Slice(Sorted Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9],
				Distance Between(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Current Array Element], Position Of(Event Player))), 0, 5),
				!Is In Line of Sight(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Current Array Element] + Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array),
				Position Of(Event Player) + Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)));
		End;
		"Goal node: the nearest visible node to the destination, taken from the start node's connected part."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[10] = First Of(Sorted Array(Array Slice(Sorted Array(
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection[11][Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]]],
			Distance Between(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Current Array Element],
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2])), 0, 5), !Is In Line of Sight(First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)
			[Current Array Element] + Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] + Vector(Empty Array,
			2, Empty Array), Barriers Do Not Block LOS)));
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3] = True;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] = First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]];
		Call Subroutine(pfUpdateNodeMeta);
		Call Subroutine(pfWalkToDestination);
		Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
		Loop If Condition Is True;
	}
}

rule("☆【PF Core】Chase target")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Event Player.pfChaseTarget != Null;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Set pfChaseTarget to a player to chase them. pfDestination follows the target while it is alive;\nUpdate & Execute only picks a new goal node once the target has moved more than 5 meters."
		If(Is Alive(Event Player.pfChaseTarget));
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[0] = Position Of(Event Player.pfChaseTarget);
		End;
		Wait(0.250, Ignore Condition);
		Loop If Condition Is True;
	}
}

rule("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Read next hop char")
{
	event
	{
		Subroutine;
		pfReadNextHopChar;
	}

	actions
	{
		"The next hop stream is cut into 128 char strings."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[14] = Index Of String Char(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3][2][Round To Integer(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[12] / 128, Down)],
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[12] % 128));
	}
}

rule("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Look up next hop (Floyd-Warshall, RLE base 1024)")
{
	event
	{
		Subroutine;
		pfLookupNextHop;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Row u of the table lists, for every destination node 0..N-1, which neighbor slot of u to walk to next.\nEach char is one run: slot * 128 + (run length - 1). Slot 6 means the destination cannot be reached.\nRow u starts at the offset stored in the first 2 cha"
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[12] = Index Of String Char(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4], Char In String(
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3][3][Round To Integer(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] / 42, Down)],
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] % 42 * 3)) * 1024 + Index Of String Char(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[4],
			Char In String(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3][3][Round To Integer(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] / 42, Down)],
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] % 42 * 3 + 1));
		"Skip whole runs until reaching the run that covers the goal node."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[13] = Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[10];
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[15] = 0;
		Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar);
		While(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[13] > Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[14] % 128);
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[13] -= Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[14] % 128 + 1;
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[12] += 1;
			Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar);
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[15] += 1;
			If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[15] % 25 == 0);
				Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition);
			End;
		End;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[16] = Round To Integer(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[14] / 128, Down);
	}
}

rule("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Progress event player")
{
	event
	{
		Subroutine;
		pfProgressEventPlayer;
	}

	actions
	{
		If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] == Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[10]);
			"Goal node reached: walk straight to the destination."
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[1] = Empty Array;
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] = -1;
		Else;
			Call Subroutine(pfLookupNextHop);
			If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[16] >= Count Of(
				Global._extendedGlobalCollection[1][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]]));
				Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0}: no path from node {1} to node {2}.", Event Player,
					Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4], Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[10]));
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[1] = Empty Array;
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] = -1;
			Else;
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[11] = Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4];
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] = First Of(
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[1][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[11]][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[16]]);
				"Attributes of the path being walked. Any rules watching these with the matching condition will activate."
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[1] = First Of(Filtered Array(
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[1][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]], First Of(Current Array Element)
					== Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[11]))[1];
			End;
		End;
		"Set the position that the player is currently walking towards."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] = Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] == -1 ? First Of(
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection) : First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)
			[Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]];
		Call Subroutine(pfUpdateNodeMeta);
	}
}

rule("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Recalibrate")
{
	event
	{
		Subroutine;
		pfRecalibrate;
	}

	actions
	{
		Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player);
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] = -1;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] = Null;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3] = False;
	}
}

rule("【PF Core】Watch progress (checked every 4 ticks)")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3] == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Replaces the Node reached, Recalibrate when off path and Check throttle rules. Their conditions changed every frame for every bot;\nthis checks the same things every 0.064 seconds. The node radius is 0.6 so a bot running at full speed cannot skip past a nod"
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[9] = False;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[20] = 0;
		While(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3]);
			If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] && Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] != -1 && Distance Between(Position Of(
				Event Player) * Vector(1, Empty Array, 1), Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] * Vector(1, Empty Array, 1))
				<= 0.600 && Absolute Value(Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)) - Y Component Of(
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5])) <= 1.500);
				"Node reached"
				Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer);
			Else If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] && Distance Between((
					Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[6] + Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5]) / 2, Event Player) > (Distance Between(
					Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[6], Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5]) + 4) * 1.200);
				"Off path"
				Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate);
			End;
			"Stuck: slower than 3 meters per second for more than 2.1 seconds."
			If(!Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] || Magnitude Of(Velocity Of(Event Player)) >= 3);
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[20] = 0;
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[9] = False;
			Else If(!Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[20]);
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[20] = Total Time Elapsed;
			Else If(Total Time Elapsed - Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[20] > 2.100);
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[9] = True;
			End;
			Wait(0.064, Ignore Condition);
		End;
	}
}

rule("【PF Core】Stop throttle when pfDestination is reset")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3] == True;
		First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection) == Null;
	}

	actions
	{
		Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player);
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3] = False;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] = Null;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] = -1;
	}
}

rule("【PF Core】Insurance")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[3] == True;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Smooth step: on the last node, go straight to the destination once it is in sight and closer."
		If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] == Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[10] && Distance Between(Event Player,
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2]) < Distance Between(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5],
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2]) && Is In Line of Sight(Position Of(Event Player) + Vector(Empty Array, 0.250,
			Empty Array), Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] + Vector(Empty Array, 0.250, Empty Array), Barriers Do Not Block LOS));
			Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer);
		Else If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] != First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection)
				&& Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] == -1 && Distance Between(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2], First Of(
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection)) < 5 && Is In Line of Sight(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] + Up, First Of(
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection) + Up, Barriers Do Not Block LOS));
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] = First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection);
		Else If(Distance Between(Event Player, Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5]) > 0.400 && !(
				!Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2] || Distance Between(First Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection),
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[2]) > 5) && (Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[9] || Distance Between(
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[6], Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5]) - 4.950 * (
				Total Time Elapsed - Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[7]) < 0) && !Has Status(Event Player, Rooted) && !Has Status(
				Event Player, Frozen) && !Has Status(Event Player, Asleep) && !Has Status(Event Player, Knocked Down) && !Has Status(
				Event Player, Stunned));
			If(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[8] - 2 == Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]);
				Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0} struggled to reach node {1} at {2}. Try adjusting your map in the editor.",
					Event Player, Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4], First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)
					[Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4]]));
				Teleport(Event Player, Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5]);
			Else;
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[8] = Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[4] + 2;
				Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate);
			End;
		End;
		Wait(2, Ignore Condition);
		Loop If Condition Is True;
	}
}

rule("【PF Test】F (Interact): spawn 20 bots that chase the host")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Event Player == Host Player;
		Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
		Has Spawned(Event Player) == True;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[10] == True;
		Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Remove the bots from the last test."
		Destroy All Dummy Bots;
		Wait(0.500, Ignore Condition);
		"Nearest node to the host: every bot spawns in the same connected part of the map, so it can reach the host."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17] = First Of(Sorted Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9], Distance Between(First Of(
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Current Array Element], Position Of(Event Player))));
		"Candidate spawn nodes: that part of the map, more than 10 meters from the host."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18] = Filtered Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9],
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Current Array Element] == Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]] && Distance Between(
			First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Current Array Element], Position Of(Event Player)) > 10);
		For Player Variable(Event Player, pfTestI, 0, 20, 1);
			"Refill with every node of that part when the candidates run out (small maps or a small part)."
			If(Count Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18]) == 0);
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18] = Filtered Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9],
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Current Array Element] == Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]]);
			End;
			"Pick a random node and remove it, so two bots do not spawn on the same node."
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17] = Random Value In Array(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18]);
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18] = Remove From Array(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18],
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]);
			"Fill the enemy team first, then the host's team."
			Create Dummy Bot(Hero(Reaper), Count Of(All Players(Opposite Team Of(Team Of(Event Player)))) < Number Of Slots(Opposite Team Of(
				Team Of(Event Player))) ? Opposite Team Of(Team Of(Event Player)) : Team Of(Event Player), -1, First Of(
				Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]], Left - Left);
			Last Created Entity.pfChaseTarget = Event Player;
			Wait(0.100, Ignore Condition);
		End;
		Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
		Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Spawned {0} of 20 test bots", Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams),
			Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))));
		Log To Inspector(Custom String("[PF Test] Spawned {0} of 20 test bots. If fewer, raise the lobby's max team players to 12.",
			Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))));
	}
}

rule("【PF Test】R (Reload): spawn 20 baseline bots that walk straight at the host (no pathfinding)")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Event Player == Host Player;
		Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False;
		Has Spawned(Event Player) == True;
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[10] == True;
		Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Remove the bots from the last test."
		Destroy All Dummy Bots;
		Wait(0.500, Ignore Condition);
		"Nearest node to the host: every bot spawns in the same connected part of the map, so it can reach the host."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17] = First Of(Sorted Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9], Distance Between(First Of(
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Current Array Element], Position Of(Event Player))));
		"Candidate spawn nodes: that part of the map, more than 10 meters from the host."
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18] = Filtered Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9],
			Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Current Array Element] == Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]] && Distance Between(
			First Of(Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Current Array Element], Position Of(Event Player)) > 10);
		For Player Variable(Event Player, pfTestI, 0, 20, 1);
			"Refill with every node of that part when the candidates run out (small maps or a small part)."
			If(Count Of(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18]) == 0);
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18] = Filtered Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection[9],
					Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Current Array Element] == Global._extendedGlobalCollection[8][Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]]);
			End;
			"Pick a random node and remove it, so two bots do not spawn on the same node."
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17] = Random Value In Array(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18]);
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18] = Remove From Array(Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[18],
				Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]);
			"Fill the enemy team first, then the host's team."
			Create Dummy Bot(Hero(Reaper), Count Of(All Players(Opposite Team Of(Team Of(Event Player)))) < Number Of Slots(Opposite Team Of(
				Team Of(Event Player))) ? Opposite Team Of(Team Of(Event Player)) : Team Of(Event Player), -1, First Of(
				Global._extendedGlobalCollection)[Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[17]], Left - Left);
			"Baseline: no pfChaseTarget, so the pathfinder never runs for this bot."
			Last Created Entity._extendedPlayerCollection[19] = True;
			Wait(0.100, Ignore Condition);
		End;
		Wait(1, Ignore Condition);
		Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Spawned {0} of 20 baseline bots (no pathfinding)", Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(
			All Teams), Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))));
	}
}

rule("【PF Test】Baseline bot: walk straight at the host")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True;
		Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[19] == True;
		Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Same movement and facing actions a pathfinding bot uses, but aimed straight at the host. The difference in server load against the F test is the cost of pathfinding."
		Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Position Of(Event Player), Position Of(Host Player)), Distance Between(
			Position Of(Event Player), Position Of(Host Player)) > 2, To World, Replace existing throttle, Direction and Magnitude);
		Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(Host Player)), 500, To World,
			Direction and Turn Rate);
	}
}

rule("☆【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Look at destination")
{
	event
	{
		Subroutine;
		pfLookAtDestination;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Calling this subroutine will make the player look to the point they are walking towards."
		Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player) * (Distance Between(Position Of(Event Player),
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5]) < 3 ? Vector(1, Empty Array, 1) : Vector(1, 1, 1)), (
			Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5] + Vector(Empty Array, 1.500, Empty Array)) * (Distance Between(Position Of(
			Event Player), Event Player._extendedPlayerCollection[5]) < 3 ? Vector(1, Empty Array, 1) : Vector(1, 1, 1))), 500, To World,
			Direction and Turn Rate);
	}
}

rule("【PF Test】Test bot: look where it walks")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Each Player;
		All;
		All;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True;
		Event Player.pfChaseTarget != Null;
		Is Alive(Event Player) == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		Call Subroutine(pfLookAtDestination);
	}
}

rule("【PF Test】Show test bot status")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Global;
	}

	actions
	{
		Create HUD Text(Host Player, Custom String("PF test | map ready: {0} | {1}", Global._extendedGlobalCollection[10], Custom String(
			"test bots: {0} | pathfinding: {1}", Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))),
			Count Of(Filtered Array(All Players(All Teams), Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)
			&& Current Array Element._extendedPlayerCollection[3])))), Null, Custom String("F (Interact) = spawn 20 test bots"), Top, 0,
			Color(Yellow), Color(White), Color(White), Visible To and String, Default Visibility);
	}
}

rule("【PF Benchmark】Server load HUD")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Global;
	}

	actions
	{
		"Shows live server load to the host. Delete this rule for a normal build."
		Enable Inspector Recording;
		Create HUD Text(Host Player, Custom String("Server load {0} | avg {1} | peak {2}", Server Load, Server Load Average,
			Server Load Peak), Null, Null, Left, 0, Color(Yellow), Color(White), Color(White), Visible To and String, Default Visibility);
	}
}

rule("【Map】Hanamura (Floyd-Warshall)")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Global;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Current Map == Map(Hanamura);
	}

	actions

rule("【Map】Havana (Floyd-Warshall)")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Global;
	}

	{
		Current Map == Map(Havana);
	}

	{

rule("【Map】NEON JUNCTION (Floyd-Warshall)")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Global;
	}

	conditions
	{
		(!Array Contains(Array(Map(Antarctic Peninsula), Map(Ayutthaya), Map(Black Forest), Map(Blizzard World), Map(Busan), Map(Castillo),
			Map(Château Guillard), Map(Circuit royal), Map(Colosseo), Map(Dorado), Map(Ecopoint: Antarctica), Map(Eichenwalde), Map(
			Esperança), Map(Hanamura), Map(Hanaoka), Map(Havana), Map(Hollywood), Map(Horizon Lunar Colony), Map(Junkertown), Map(
			Kanezaka), Map(King's Row), Map(Lijiang Tower), Map(Malevento), Map(Midtown), Map(Necropolis), Map(New Queen Street), Map(
			Numbani), Map(Oasis), Map(Paraíso), Map(Paris), Map(Petra), Map(Practice Range), Map(Rialto), Map(Route 66), Map(Runasapi),
			Map(Samoa), Map(Shambali Monastery), Map(Temple of Anubis), Map(Throne of Anubis), Map(Volskaya Industries), Map(
			Watchpoint: Gibraltar)), Current Map) && Distance Between(Ray Cast Hit Position(Vector(-33.850, 2.370, 234.790), Vector(
			-33.850, -0.630, 234.790), Null, Null, False), Vector(-33.850, 1.370, 234.790)) < 1.500 && Distance Between(
			Ray Cast Hit Position(Vector(-7.310, 3.250, 216.430), Vector(-7.310, 0.250, 216.430), Null, Null, False), Vector(-7.310, 2.250,
			216.430)) < 1.500) == True;
	}

	actions
	{
		Global._extendedGlobalCollection[3] = Array(Array(Custom String(
			"޸ौ޼̇ࠖ୒޹޼˸࠘ং޺݀޼࠘͟޺ਿ޼͍ࠖ֙޺÷޼̏ࠗ˯޸୙޼̉߿৽޹֖޼͟߾୥޺̈́޼߰߾ࡴ޺࢚޼੄߿۔޺ࢌ޼̝ࠗ͞޻֜޼͟߿̉޼͓޼͟߿˺޻৤޼͟߾ܲ޻̟޼͟߾઒޻ࣕ޼͐ࠖް޼ࣿ޼͐߿॔޽֭޼׸ִࠖ޽׫޼ࠖ͟਋޻̞޼࠘̿͟޻޼ۚ࠘௎޻߰޼ۚ࠙)"),

rule("【Map】Paraíso (Floyd-Warshall)")
{
	event
	{
		Ongoing - Global;
	}

	conditions
	{
		Current Map == Map(Paraíso);
	}

	actions
